AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text="Fixed. The build passes (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`).\n\n## Root cause\n\nOne file: `app/src/main/java/org/sufficientlysecure/htmltextview/LocalLinkMovementMethod.java`.\n\nComment bodies are `HtmlTextView`s, which install `LocalLinkMovementMethod` as their movement method (`HtmlTextView.java:114`). A long tap on a comment therefore ran **two independent handlers off the same gesture**:\n\n1. `TextView`'s inherited `View.onTouchEvent` detects the long press after ~500 ms and fires the `OnLongClickListener` set in `CommentsRecyclerViewAdapter.ItemViewHolder` (line 428) → the comment menu dialog opens.\n2. When the finger lifts, `TextView.onTouchEvent` still forwards the `ACTION_UP` to the movement method, and `LocalLinkMovementMethod` called `link[0].onClick(widget)` **unconditionally** — with no check of how long the press lasted. That reached the adapter's `setOnClickATagListener` → `Utils.openLinkMaybeHN(...)` → the URL opened on top of the dialog.\n\n`View` itself guards against this for normal clicks (it sets `mHasPerformedLongPress` and suppresses the click), but the movement method had no equivalent guard, so the tap-vs-long-tap distinction was simply never made for links.\n\n## The fix\n\nOn `ACTION_UP`, the link's `onClick` is skipped when the gesture already qualified as a long press:\n\n```java\nif (event.getEventTime() - event.getDownTime() >= ViewConfiguration.getLongPressTimeout())\n```\n\nUsing the `MotionEvent`'s own `downTime`/`eventTime` keeps the check **stateless**, which matters here because `LocalLinkMovementMethod` is a process-wide singleton (`sInstance`) shared by every `HtmlTextView`; storing the press start in a field would let concurrently-touched views corrupt each other's state. Comparing against the system `getLongPressTimeout()` also means the threshold automatically tracks the user's accessibility setting, staying in sync with whatever `View` used to decide to fire the long click.\n\nTwo small robustness additions in the same touch path:\n- `ACTION_UP` now clears the pressed-link highlight, which the original code left stuck on after release.\n- `ACTION_CANCEL` is handled explicitly, so a link highlighted on `ACTION_DOWN` that then gets taken over by the RecyclerView's scroll no longer stays highlighted.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Short tap on a URL → opens as before (unchanged).\n- Long tap on a URL → comment menu opens, URL does **not** open.\n- Long tap elsewhere in a comment, taps on non-link text, and the story-header links (which share this movement method) are all unaffected.\n\nOne note: I checked whether the fix belonged in the adapter instead, and the movement method is the right place — the header text in `HeaderViewHolder` routes links through the same class, so both link surfaces are covered by the single change. `UserDialogFragment` uses the platform `LinkMovementMethod`, but it has no long-click listener competing for the gesture, so it has no equivalent bug and needed no change.")], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 100, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 36408, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 100, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 2, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAGgP81p1ojtAmcgk3z5', stop_reason=None, session_id='77ae3570-94ca-4478-bd01-ee90904336ca', uuid='68b5c3cb-3335-407b-8998-5f655b4d2cb2')
